I 


This  publication  contains  the  alphabetical  list  by  issuers  of  all  securi¬ 
ties  admitted  to  trading  on  stock  exchanges  under  the  Securities  Exchange  Act 
of  1934  except  securities  exempted  under  Section  3(a) (12)  of  the  Act,  such  as 
obligations  of  the  United  States  Government,  States  and  political  subdivisions 
thereof.  "Traded"  and  "admitted  to  trading"  as  used  in  this  publication  refer 
to  the  statutory  privilege  of  trading  on  exchanges  under  the  Act;  therefore, 
issues  which  may  be  suspended  from  trading  are  included.  This  list  does  not 
include  banks,  whose  securities  are  traded  on  exchanges,  which  are  subject  to 
the  jurisdiction  of  the  Office  of  the  Controller,  the  Board  of  Governors  of  the 
Federal  Reserve  System  or  the  Federal  Deposit  Insurance  Corporation. 

For  the  purpose  of  condensation  certain  standard  abbreviations  are  used: 
Stock  Exchanges  registered  under  the  Act  are  abbreviated  as  follows: 


AMS  -  AMERICAN  STOCK  EXCHANGE

Securities  are  identified  in  this  publication  by  their  principal 
characteristics,  not  necessarily  the  "official"  title.  The  word  "stock"  is 
generally  omitted  following  the  description  "common  "  t  "capital",  "preferred" 
and  "preference." 

The  abbreviation  of  the  legal  status  under  which  securities  have 
been  admitted  to  trading  on  exchanges  is  as  follows: 

R  Securities  registered 

X  Securities  temporarily  exempted  from  registration  * 

U  Securities  admitted  to  unlisted  trading  privileges 

XL  Securities  listed  on  an  exempted  exchange 

XU  Securities  admitted  to  unlisted  trading  privileges 

on  an  exempted  exchange. 

*  The  Commission's  rule  under  which  such  securities 
are  temporarily  exempted  from  registration  is 
given  immediately  below  the  security  description. 

The  basic  registration  form  and  the  Commission's  file  number  are 
shown  for  registered  securities.  Applications  for  registration  and  the 
annual  and  other  periodic  reports  relating  to  registered  issues  may  be  seen 
at  the  offices  of  the  exchange  on  which  the  securities  are  registered,  and 
at  the  Commission's  Public  Reference  Room  in  Washington,  D.  C.  Photocopies 
may  be  purchased  from  the  Commission;  inquiries  concerning  photocopies  should 
be  addressed:  Securities  and  Exchange  Commission,  Washington,  D.  C.,  20549. 

Securities  added  to  this  list  or  removed  therefrom  and  changes  and 
corrections  made  in  the  list  since  December  31,  1970,  will  be  shown  in  four 
quarterly  supplements  to  be  published  as  of  the  end  of  each  quarter  beginning 
with  the  quarter  ended  March  31,  1971.

This  publication  contains  the  alphabetical  list  by  issuers  of  all  securi¬ 
ties  admitted  to  trading  on  stock  exchanges  under  the  Securities  Exchange  Act 
of  1934  except  securities  exempted  under  Section  3(a)(12)  of  the  Act,  such  as 
obligations  of  the  United  States  Government,  States  and  political  subdivisions 
thereof.  "Traded"  and  "admitted  to  trading"  as  used  in  this  publication  refer 
to  the  statutory  privilege  of  trading  on  exchanges  under  the  Act;  therefore, 
issues  which  may  be  suspended  from  trading  are  included.  This  list  does  not 
include  banks,  whose  securities  are  traded  on  exchanges,  which  are  subject  to 
the  jurisdiction  of  the  Office  of  the  Controller,  the  Board  of  Governors  of  the 
Federal  Reserve  System  or  the  Federal  Deposit  Insurance  Corporation. 

For  the  purpose  of  condensation  certain  standard  abbreviations  are  used: 
Stock  Exchanges  registered  under  the  Act  are  abbreviated  as  follows:

Securities  are  identified  in  this  publication  by  their  principal 
characteristics,  not  necessarily  the  "official"  title.  The  word  "stock"  is 
generally  omitted  following  the  description  "common",  "capital",  "preferred" 
and  "preference." 

The  abbreviation  of  the  legal  status  under  which  securities  have 
been  admitted  to  trading  on  exchanges  is  as  follows: 

R  Securities  registered 

X  Securities  temporarily  exempted  from  registration  * 

U  Securities  admitted  to  unlisted  trading  privileges 

XL  Securities  listed  on  an  exempted  exchange 

XU  Securities  admitted  to  unlisted  trading  privileges 

on  an  exempted  exchange. 

*  The  Commission' s  rule  under  which  such  securities 
are  temporarily  exempted  from  registration  is 
given  immediately  below  the  security  description. 

The  basic  registration  form  and  the  Commission's  file  number  are 
shown  for  registered  securities.  Applications  for  registration  and  the 
annual  and  other  periodic  reports  relating  to  registered  issues  may  be  seen 
at  the  offices  of  the  exchange  on  which  the  securities  are  registered,  and 
at  the  Commission's  Public  Reference  Room  in  Washington,  D.  C.  Photocopies 
may  be  purchased  from  the  Commission;  inquiries  concerning  photocopies  should 
be  addressed:  Securities  and  Exchange  Commission,  Washington,  D.  C.,  20549. 

Securities  added  to  this  list  or  removed  therefrom  and  changes  and 
corrections  made  in  the  list  since  December  31,  1971,  will  be  shown  in  four 
quarterly  supplements  to  be  published  as  of  the  end  of  each  quarter  beginning 
with  the  quarter  ended  March  31,  1972.
